 'type', c.COLUMN_TYPE, 'data_type', c.DATA_TYPE, 'nullable', c.IS_NULLABLE, 'default', c.COLUMN_DEFAULT,\n      'extra', c.EXTRA, 'generation', c.GENERATION_EXPRESSION, 'charset', JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT('name', x.INDEX_NAME, 'seq', x.SEQ_IN_INDEX,\n      'column', x.COLUMN_NAME, 'expression', NULL, 'sub_part', x.SUB_PART, 'collation', x.COLLATION,\n      'non_unique', x.NON_UNIQUE, 'type', x.INDEX_TYPE, 'visible', x.IS_VISIBLE))\n    FROM information_schema.STATISTICS x WHERE x.TABLE_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND x.TABLE_NAME = 'items'),\n  'foreign_keys', (SELECT JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT('name', k.CONSTRAINT_NAME, 'position', k.ORDINAL_POSITION,\n      'column', k.COLUMN_NAME, 'ref_schema', k.REFERENCED_TABLE_SCHEMA, 'ref_table', k.REFERENCED_TABLE_NAME,\n      'ref_column', k.REFERENCED_COLUMN_NAME, 'on_delete', r.DELETE_RULE, 'on_update', r.UPDATE_RULE))\n    FROM information_schema.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE k JOIN (SELECT r.CONSTRAINT_NAME, r.DELETE_RULE, r.UPDATE_RULE\n      FROM information_schema.REFERENTIAL_CONSTRAINTS r\n      WHERE r.CONSTRAINT_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND r.TABLE_NAME = 'items') r ON r.CONSTRAINT_NAME = k.CONSTRAINT_NAME\n    WHERE k.TABLE_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND k.TABLE_NAME = 'items' AND k.REFERENCED_TABLE_NAME IS NOT NULL),\n  'checks', NULL,\n  'triggers', (SELECT JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT('name', g.TRIGGER_NAME, 'timing', g.ACTION_TIMING,\n      'event', g.EVENT_MANIPULATION, 'orientation', g.ACTION_ORIENTATION, 'statement', g.ACTION_STATEMENT,\n      'order', g.ACTION_ORDER))\n    FROM information_schema.TRIGGERS g\n    WHERE g.EVENT_OBJECT_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND g.EVENT_OBJECT_TABLE = 'items'),\n  'trigger_privilege', EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.USER_PRIVILEGES p\n      WHERE p.PRIVILEGE_TYPE = 'TRIGGER' AND p.GRANTEE = CONCAT('''', LEFT(CURRENT_USER(), CHAR_LENGTH(CURRENT_USER()) - CHAR_LENGTH(SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1)) - 1), '''@''', SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1), ''''))\n CHAR_LENGTH(SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1)) - 1), '''@''', SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1), '''') AND 'shop' LIKE p.TABLE_SCHEMA)\n    OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.TABLE_PRIVILEGES p\n      WHERE p.PRIVILEGE_TYPE = 'TRIGGER' AND p.GRANTEE = CONCAT('''', LEFT(CURRENT_USER(), CHAR_LENGTH(CURRENT_USER()) - CHAR_LENGTH(SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1)) - 1), '''@''', SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1), '''')\n      AND p.TABLE_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND p.TABLE_NAME = 'items'),\n  'column_privilege', ((0 = 0 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.USER_PRIVILEGES p\n      WHERE p.PRIVILEGE_TYPE IN ('SELECT', 'INSERT', 'UPDATE', 'REFERENCES') AND CAST(p.GRANTEE AS BINARY) = BINARY)) FROM information_schema.SCHEMA_PRIVILEGES p\n        WHERE CAST(p.GRANTEE AS BINARY) = CAST(CONCAT('''', LEFT(CURRENT_USER(), CHAR_LENGTH(CURRENT_USER()) - structure MySQL 8.0.15 default "shop"."items": "SELECT JSON_OBJECT(\n  'kind', t.TABLE_TYPE,\n  'columns', (SELECT JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT('position', c.ORDINAL_POSITION, 'name', c.COLUMN_NAME,\n      'type', c.COLUMN_TYPE, 'data_type', c.DATA_TYPE, 'nullable', c.IS_NULLABLE, 'default', c.COLUMN_DEFAULT,\n      'extra', c.EXTRA, 'generation', c.GENERATION_EXPRESSION, 'charset', c.CHARACTER_SET_NAME,\n      'collation', c.COLLATION_NAME))\n    FROM information_schema.COLUMNS c WHERE c.TABLE_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND c.TABLE_NAME = 'items'),\n  'indexes', (SELECT JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT('name', x.INDEX_NAME, 'seq', x.SEQ_IN_INDEX,\n      'column', x.COLUMN_NAME, 'expression', x.EXPRESSION, 'sub_part', x.SUB_PART, 'collation', x.COLLATION,\n      'non_unique', x.NON_UNIQUE, 'type', x.INDEX_TYPE, 'visible', x.IS_VISIBLE))\n    FROM information_schema.STATISTICS x WHERE x.TABLE_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND x.TABLE_NAME = 'items'),\n  'foreign_keys', (SELECT JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT('name', k.CONSTRAINT_NAME, 'position', k.ORDINAL_POSITION,\n      'column', k.COLUMN_NAME, 'ref_schema', k.REFERENCED_TABLE_SCHEMA, 'ref_table', k.REFERENCED_TABLE_NAME,\n      'ref_column', k.REFERENCED_COLUMN_NAME, 'on_delete', r.DELETE_RULE, 'on_update', r.UPDATE_RULE))\n    FROM information_schema.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE k JOIN (SELECT r.CONSTRAINT_NAME, r.DELETE_RULE, r.UPDATE_RULE\n      FROM information_schema.REFERENTIAL_CONSTRAINTS r\n      WHERE r.CONSTRAINT_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND r.TABLE_NAME = 'items') r ON r.CONSTRAINT_NAME = k.CONSTRAINT_NAME\n    WHERE k.TABLE_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND k.TABLE_NAME = 'items' AND k.REFERENCED_TABLE_NAME IS NOT NULL),\n  'checks', NULL,\n  'triggers', (SELECT JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT('name', g.TRIGGER_NAME, 'timing', g.ACTION_TIMING,\n      'event', g.EVENT_MANIPULATION, 'orientation', g.ACTION_ORIENTATION, 'statement', g.ACTION_STATEMENT,\n      'order', g.ACTION_ORDER))\n    FROM information_schema.TRIGGERS g\n    WHERE g.EVENT_OBJECT_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND g.EVENT_OBJECT_TABLE = 'items'),\n  'trigger_privilege', EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.USER_PRIVILEGES p\n      WHERE p.PRIVILEGE_TYPE = 'TRIGGER' AND p.GRANTEE = CONCAT('''', LEFT(CURRENT_USER(), CHAR_LENGTH(CURRENT_USER()) - CHAR_LENGTH(SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1)) - 1), '''@''', SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1), ''''))\n    OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.SCHEMA_PRIVILEGES p\n      WHERE p.PRIVILEGE_TYPE = 'TRIGGER' AND p.GRANTEE = CONCAT('''', LEFT(CURRENT_USER(), CHAR_LENGTH(CURRENT_USER()) - CHAR_LENGTH(SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1)) - 1), '''@''', SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1), '''') AND 'shop' LIKE p.TABLE_SCHEMA)\n    OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.TABLE_PRIVILEGES p\n      WHERE p.PRIVILEGE_TYPE = 'TRIGGER' AND p.GRANTEE = CONCAT('''', LEFT(CURRENT_USER(), CHAR_LENGTH(CURRENT_USER()) - CHAR_LENGTH(SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1)) - 1), '''@''', SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1), '''')\n      AND p.TABLE_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND p.TABLE_NAME = 'items'),\n  'column_privilege', ((0 = 0 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.USER_PRIVILEGES p\n      WHERE p.PRIVILEGE_TYPE IN ('SELECT', 'INSERT', 'UPDATE', 'REFERENCES') AND CAST(p.GRANTEE AS BINARY) = CAST(CONCAT('''', LEFT(CURRENT_USER(), CHAR_LENGTH(CURRENT_USER()) - CHAR_LENGTH(SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1)) - 1), '''@''', SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1), '''') AS BINARY)))\n    OR (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.SCHEMA_PRIVILEGES p\n        WHERE p.PRIVILEGE_TYPE IN ('SELECT', 'INSERT', 'UPDATE', 'REFERENCES') AND CAST(p.GRANTEE AS BINARY) = CAST(CONCAT('''', LEFT(CURRENT_USER(), CHAR_LENGTH(CURRENT_USER()) - CHAR_LENGTH(SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1)) - 1), '''@''', SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1), '''') AS BINARY)\n        AND IF(0 = 0, CAST('shop' AS BINARY) LIKE CAST(p.TABLE_SCHEMA AS BINARY) ESCAPE '\\\\',\n        CAST(p.TABLE_SCHEMA AS BINARY) = CAST('shop' AS BINARY)))\n      AND (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT CAST(p.TABLE_SCHEMA AS BINARY)) FROM information_schema.SCHEMA_PRIVILEGES p\n        WHERE CAST(p.GRANTEE AS BINARY) = CAST(CONCAT('''', LEFT(CURRENT_USER(), CHAR_LENGTH(CURRENT_USER()) - CHAR_LENGTH(SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1)) - 1), '''@''', SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1), '''') AS BINARY) AND IF(0 = 0, CAST('shop' AS BINARY) LIKE CAST(p.TABLE_SCHEMA AS BINARY) ESCAPE '\\\\',\n        CAST(p.TABLE_SCHEMA AS BINARY) = CAST('shop' AS BINARY))) = 1)\n    OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.TABLE_PRIVILEGES p\n      WHERE p.PRIVILEGE_TYPE IN ('SELECT', 'INSERT', 'UPDATE', 'REFERENCES') AND CAST(p.GRANTEE AS BINARY) = CAST(CONCAT('''', LEFT(CURRENT_USER(), CHAR_LENGTH(CURRENT_USER()) - CHAR_LENGTH(SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1)) - 1), '''@''', SUBSTRING_INDEX(CURRENT_USER(), '@', -1), '''') AS BINARY)\n      AND CAST(p.TABLE_SCHEMA AS BINARY) = CAST('shop' AS BINARY)\n      AND CAST(p.TABLE_NAME AS BINARY) = CAST('items' AS BINARY))))\nFROM information_schema.TABLES t WHERE t.TABLE_SCHEMA = 'shop' AND t.TABLE_NAME = 'items'"
